# Proposed OEIS comments: A251977 to A253452

One section per OEIS entry: the date the result was obtained, the paper that proves it, what the entry still records as unsettled, and the text proposed for posting on that entry.

**Nothing here has been posted.** See [../SUBMITTING.md](../SUBMITTING.md).

---

## A251977

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A251977>  
Result obtained: **3 September 2026**  
Paper: [4437](../papers/04001-04500/04437-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 10*a(n-1) -32*a(n-2) +92*a(n-3) -115*a(n-4) -382*a(n-5) -27*a(n-6) -1693*a(n-7) +2909*a(n-8) +6402*a(n-9) -2547*a(n-10) -18945*a(n-11) +26884*a(n-12) +59073*a(n-13) +105052*a(n-14) -227872*a(n-15) -270075*a(n-16) -345912*a(n-17) +303731*a(n-18) +608913*a(n-19) +788586*a(n-20) -309853*a(n-21) -327886*a(n-22) -1130785*a(n-23) -36733*a(n-24) -87267*a(n-25) +639150*a(n-26) +896823*a(n-27) -142377*a(n-28) +277511*a(n-29) -1213349*a(n-30) +374720*a(n-31) -525110*a(n-32) +797496*a(n-33) -303295*a(n-34) +233053*a(n-35) -277630*a(n-36) +103222*a(n-37) -25724*a(n-38) +40850*a(n-39) -16767*a(n-40) +3808*a(n-41) -8339*a(n-42) +6700*a(n-43) -3289*a(n-44) +1526*a(n-45) -616*a(n-46) +110*a(n-47) for n>53

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 65 793 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 53. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A252544>  
Result obtained: **1 September 2026**  
Paper: [2521](../papers/02501-03000/02521-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: T(n,k) is symmetric for n and k both greater than 1.
> Empirical for column k:
> Empirical for row n:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical column recurrences are true. Each column counts arrays of a fixed width, and counting those a row at a time gives a transfer matrix, so every column satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most the number of states; checking the ones above is then a finite exact computation, and they hold. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A253430

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A253430>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [9501](../papers/09501-10000/09501-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 3*a(n-1) - 2*a(n-2) for n>4.
> Empirical: a(n) = 9*2^(n-1) + 49 for n>2.
> Empirical g.f.: x*(69 - 137*x + 13*x^2 + 6*x^3) / ((1 - x)*(1 - 2*x)). - _Colin Barker_, Dec 12 2018

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 69 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 4. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

> Empirical for row n:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical column recurrences are true. Each column counts arrays of a fixed width, and counting those a row at a time gives a transfer matrix, so every column satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most the number of states; checking the ones above is then a finite exact computation, and they hold. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```
